            f.write("""// Firebase Cloud Functions for Agape Sovereign
const functions = require('firebase-functions');
const admin = require('firebase-admin');
const bigquery = require('@google-cloud/bigquery');

admin.initializeApp();
const db = admin.firestore();

// Initialize BigQuery client
const bq = bigquery({
  projectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
  key: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY
});

// Main export
exports.initiateDIFFScan = functions.https.onCall(async (data, context) => {
  // Check if user is authenticated
  if (!context.auth) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError('unauthenticated', 'User must be authenticated');
  }

  const userId = context.auth.uid;
  const userEmail = context.auth.token.email;

  // Start DIFF scan
  const scanId = functions.runtime.apiId().split('-')[4] + '-' + Date.now();
  const scanData = {
    userId,
    userEmail,
    scanId,
    status: 'initiated',
    createdAt: new Date(),
    results: {}
  };

  // Save scan to Firestore
  await db.collection('scans').doc(scanId).set(scanData);

  // Trigger breach scanning
  await scanBreach(emailUser);

  return scanId;
});

// Breach scanning function
async function scanBreach(email) {
  // TODO: Implement breach scanning logic
  // This would use breach databases or API services
  console.log('Scanning breach for:', email);
}

// Health check endpoint
exports.healthCheck = functions.https.onRequest(async (context, req) => {
  return {
    status: 'healthy',
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    service: 'agape-sovereign'
  };
});
""")
        
        functions_auth = os.path.join(functions_dir, "auth.js")
        with open(functions_auth, "w") as f:
            f.write("""// Firebase Cloud Functions for Authentication
const functions = require('firebase-functions');
const admin = require('firebase-admin');
const crypto = require('crypto');

admin.initializeApp();
const db = admin.firestore();

// Register passkey
exports.registerPasskey = functions.https.onCall(async (data, context) => {
  if (!context.auth) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError('unauthenticated', 'User must be authenticated');
  }

  const userId = context.auth.uid;
  const { challenge, publicKey, authenticatorData } = data;

  // Verify WebAuthn assertion
  const signature = crypto.verify(
    challenge,
    authenticatorData,
    {
      signature: crypto.createVerificationKey('sha-256').export(),
      publicKey
    }
  );

  if (!signature) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError('invalid-arg', 'Invalid signature');
  }

  // Store passkey credential
  await db.collection('users').doc(userId).set({
    passkeyRegistered: true,
    passkeyRegisteredAt: new Date()
  }, { merge: true });

  return { success: true };
});

// Verify passkey login
exports.verifyPasskeyLogin = functions.https.onCall(async (data, context) => {
  if (!context.auth) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError('unauthenticated', 'User must be authenticated');
  }

  const userId = context.auth.uid;
  const { assertion } = data;

  // Verify passkey assertion
  const signature = crypto.verify(
    assertion,
    assertion,
    {
      signature: crypto.createVerificationKey('sha-256').export(),
      publicKey: assertion.publicKey
    }
  );

  if (!signature) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError('invalid-arg', 'Invalid signature');
  }

  return { success: true };
});
""")
        
        functions_breach = os.path.join(functions_dir, "breach.js")
        with open(functions_breach, "w") as f:
            f.write("""// Firebase Cloud Functions for Breach Detection
const functions = require('firebase-functions');
const admin = require('firebase-admin');
const axios = require('axios');

admin.initializeApp();
const db = admin.firestore();

// Check for email breach
exports.checkBreach = functions.https.onRequest(async (context, req) => {
  if (!context.auth) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError('unauthenticated', 'User must be authenticated');
  }

  const userEmail = context.auth.token.email;
  const query = req.query.email || userEmail;

  // Check breach databases
  const breachResults = await checkBreachDatabases(query);

  // Save results to Firestore
  await db.collection('breaches').add({
    email: query,
    breached: breachResults.breached,
    breachCount: breachResults.count,
    breachedAt: breachResults.breachDetails.map(b => b.breachName),
    checkedAt: new Date(),
    userId: context.auth.uid
  });

  return {
    breached: breachResults.breached,
    count: breachResults.count,
    details: breachResults.breachDetails
  };
});

// Check breach databases
async function checkBreachDatabases(email) {
  const breached = [];
  const breachDetails = [];

  // TODO: Implement breach database checks
  // This would use services like HaveIBeenPwned, DataBase, etc.
  
  // Example: Check HaveIBeenPwned API
  // const response = await axios.get(`https://haveibeenpwned.com/api/v3/breachedaccount/${email}`);
  // if (response.data.BreachedAccounts.length > 0) {
  //   breached = true;
  //   breachDetails.push(response.data.BreachedAccounts.map(a => ({
  //     breachName: a.BreachName,
  //     breachDate: a.BreachDate
  //   })));
  // }

  return {
    breached: false,
    count: 0,
    breachDetails: []
  };
}
""")
        
        functions_report = os.path.join(functions_dir, "report.js")
        with open(functions_report, "w") as f:
            f.write("""// Firebase Cloud Functions for Report Generation
const functions = require('firebase-functions');
const admin = require('firebase-admin');
const PDFDocument = require('pdfkit');

admin.initializeApp();
const db = admin.firestore();

// Generate breach report
exports.generateBreachReport = functions.firestore
  .document('users/{userId}')
  .onWrite(async (change, context) => {
    if (change.type === 'create' || change.after.passkeyRegistered) {
      const userId = change.after.id;
      const userEmail = context.auth.token.email;

      // Generate report
      const reportId = 'report-' + Date.now();
      const reportData = {
        userId,
        userEmail,
        reportId,
        generatedAt: new Date(),
        status: 'generating'
      };

      await db.collection('reports').doc(reportId).set(reportData);

      // Generate PDF (TODO: Implement PDF generation)
      // await generatePDF(reportId, userEmail);

      return reportData;
    }
  });

// Generate PDF report
async function generatePDF(reportId, userEmail) {
  const doc = new PDFDocument();
  const file = await new Promise((resolve) => {
    const stream = createWriteStream(`/tmp/${reportId}.pdf`);
    doc.pipe(stream);
    stream.on('close', resolve);
  });

  doc.text('Agape Sovereign Breach Report', { fontSize: 36 });
  doc.text(`Email: ${userEmail}`, { fontSize: 24 });
  doc.text(`Generated: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`, { fontSize: 18 });

  // TODO: Add more report content
  // Add breach details, recommendations, etc.

  doc.end();
}
""")
        
        functions_cleanup = os.path.join(functions_dir, "cleanup.js")
        with open(functions_cleanup, "w") as f:
            f.write("""// Firebase Cloud Functions for Cleanup
const functions = require('firebase-functions');
const admin = require('firebase-admin');

admin.initializeApp();
const db = admin.firestore();

// Cleanup old reports (older than 2 years)
exports.cleanupOldReports = functions.pubsub.schedule('0 0 * * *').run(async () => {
  const cutoffDate = new Date(Date.now() - 2 * 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  
  const reportsSnapshot = await db.collection('reports')
    .where('generatedAt', '<=', cutoffDate)
    .get();

  const deletePromises = reportsSnapshot.docs.map(doc => {
    return db.collection('reports').doc(doc.path).delete().then(() => {
      // Also delete associated audit logs
      return db.collection('audit_logs').where('reportId', '==', doc.data().reportId)
        .get()
        .then(snapshot => {
          snapshot.forEach(logDoc => {
            return db.collection('audit_logs').doc(logDoc.path).delete();
          });
        });
    });
  });

  await Promise.all(deletePromises);
  console.log('Cleaned up old reports');
});
""")
        
        functions_health = os.path.join(functions_dir, "health.js")
        with open(functions_health, "w") as f:
            f.write("""// Firebase Cloud Functions for Health Check
const functions = require('firebase-functions');

// Health check endpoint
exports.healthCheck = functions.https.onRequest(async (context, req) => {
  return {
    status: 'healthy',
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    service: 'agape-sovereign',
    uptime: process.uptime(),
    memory: process.memoryUsage(),
    nodeVersion: process.version
  };
});
""")
        
        functions_bigquery = os.path.join(functions_dir, "bigquery.js")
        with open(functions_bigquery, "w") as f:
            f.write("""// Firebase Cloud Functions for BigQuery
const functions = require('firebase-functions');
const bigquery = require('@google-cloud/bigquery');

const bq = bigquery({
  projectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
  key: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY
});

// Create audit log table
exports.createAuditLogTable = functions.pubsub.schedule('0 0 * * *').run(async () => {
  const tableId = `agape-sovereign.audit_logs`;
  
  await bq.createTable(tableId, {
    schema: [
      {name: 'logId', type: 'string'},
      {name: 'userId', type: 'string'},
      {name: 'email', type: 'string'},
      {name: 'action', type: 'string'},
      {name: 'timestamp', type: 'timestamp'},
      {name: 'metadata', type: 'json'}
    ]
  });
});

// Load audit logs into BigQuery
exports.loadAuditLogsToBigQuery = functions.pubsub.schedule('0 12 * * *').run(async () => {
  const auditLogsSnapshot = await db.collection('audit_logs').get();
  
  const rows = auditLogsSnapshot.docs.map(doc => ({
    logId: doc.data().logId,
    userId: doc.data().userId,
    email: doc.data().email,
    action: doc.data().action,
    timestamp: doc.data().timestamp.toDate(),
    metadata: doc.data().metadata
  }));

  await bq.insertAll('agape-sovereign.audit_logs', rows);
});
""")
    
    print("✅ Cloud Functions created")

      functions_breach,

## Prerequisites

1. **Node.js 20+** - [Download](https://nodejs.org/)
2. **Firebase CLI** - `npm install -g firebase-tools`
3. **Google Cloud Project** - Create in [Firebase Console](https://console.firebase.google.com/)
4. **Git** - [Download](https://git-scm.com/)
5. **Docker** - [Download](https://www.docker.com/) (optional)

## Quick Setup

### 1. Clone Repository

```bash
git clone https://github.com/izrl613/agape-sovereign.git
cd agape-sovereign
```

### 2. Install Dependencies

```bash
npm install
```

### 3. Configure Environment Variables

```bash

# Edit .env with your Firebase credentials
```

### 4. Deploy to Firebase

```bash
# Login to Firebase
firebase login

# Deploy all services
npm run deploy
```

## Firebase Configuration

### Create Firebase Project

1. Go to [Firebase Console](https://console.firebase.google.com/)
2. Click "Add Project"
3. Enter project name: `agape-sovereign`
4. Enable **Authentication** (Google OAuth, Passkeys)
5. Enable **Firestore Database**
6. Enable **Cloud Functions**
7. Enable **Hosting**
8. Enable **Storage**

### Get Firebase Configuration

From Firebase Console > Project Settings > General > Your apps:
- API Key
- Auth Domain
- Project ID
- Storage Bucket
- Messaging Sender ID
- App ID
- Measurement ID

### Configure Firebase

```bash
# Initialize Firebase CLI
firebase init

# Select services: hosting, firestore, functions, storage
# Project ID: agape-sovereign
# Public directory: public
# Storage configuration: Use default
# Functions configuration: Use default
```

```bash
npm run deploy:frontend
```

This deploys:
- Static files to Firebase Hosting
- Global CDN distribution
- Automatic HTTPS
- Custom domain support

### Deploy Cloud Functions

```bash
npm run deploy:functions

This deploys:
- Node.js 22 Cloud Functions
- Authentication handlers
- Breach detection functions
- Report generation functions
- Cleanup functions

### Deploy Database Rules

```bash
npm run deploy:rules
```

This deploys:
- Firestore security rules
- Storage security rules
- Index configurations

### Deploy Complete Stack

```bash
npm run deploy
```

## Docker Deployment

### Build and Run Locally

```bash
docker-compose up --build
```

### Run in Production

```bash
docker-compose up -d
```

### Docker Compose Services

- **app**: Next.js application
- **db**: PostgreSQL database

## Monitoring

### View Logs

```bash
# Firebase CLI logs
firebase functions:logs

# Docker logs
docker-compose logs -f
```

### Health Check

```bash
curl https://your-app-url.com/api/health
```

## Troubleshooting

### Common Issues

1. **Firebase Authentication Error**
   - Check Firebase Console authentication providers
   - Verify .env configuration
   - Check Firebase CLI version

2. **Database Connection Error**
   - Verify DATABASE_URL in .env
   - Check PostgreSQL connection
   - Ensure port 5432 is open

3. **Build Errors**
   - Clear node_modules and reinstall
   - Check TypeScript configuration
   - Verify dependencies

## Security Best Practices

1. **Never commit .env files** - Use .gitignore
2. **Use Firebase App Check** - Prevent unauthorized access
3. **Enable Firebase App Check** in Hosting
4. **Use secure keys** - Rotate regularly
5. **Enable 2FA** - For Firebase Admin access
6. **Review security rules** - Regular audits

## Support

For support, email support@agape-sovereign.com or join our community Discord.

MIT License - Free for personal and commercial use.

# 🚀 Architect AI — Complete Deployment Guide

## Agape Sovereign Enclave 2026 | DIFF Intelligence Platform

This guide will walk you through deploying Architect AI on Firebase free tier with your existing credentials.

---

## ✅ Pre-Deployment Checklist

- [ ] Firebase project created: **agape-sovereign**
- [ ] Node.js 20+ installed
- [ ] Firebase CLI installed (`npm install -g firebase-tools`)
- [ ] Gemini API key obtained
- [ ] Admin emails configured (`idin@agape.nyc`, `agape@sovereign.nyc`)
- [ ] Domain configured (optional): `agape-sovereign.nyc`

---

## 🔑 Your Firebase Credentials

```
📊 Firebase Console: https://console.firebase.google.com/project/agape-sovereign

Project ID: agape-sovereign
Auth Domain: agape-sovereign.firebaseapp.com
Storage Bucket: agape-sovereign.firebasestorage.app
Messaging Sender ID: 956088455461
App ID: 1:956088455461:web:5d83545efc8961e4904acc
Measurement ID: G-6YG9BGTWDD
```

---

## 📦 Step 1: Clone & Install Dependencies

```bash
# Clone the repository
cd /Users/aarondavid/Documents/GitHub
git clone https://github.com/izrl613/agape-sovereign.git
cd firebase

# Install root dependencies
npm install

# Install Cloud Functions dependencies
cd functions
npm install
cd ..
```

**Expected Output:**
```
✓ next@15.x.x
✓ react@19.x.x
✓ firebase@10.x.x
✓ firebase-admin@12.x.x
✓ firebase-functions@5.x.x
```

---

## 🔧 Step 2: Configure Environment Variables

### Create `.env.local`

```bash
# Copy template
cp .env.local.template .env.local
